2 (10) (a) THE COURT MAY, UPON A SHOWING OF GOOD CAUSE,
3 ORDER CDHS TO FACILITATE AN EXAMINATION BY A PROFESSIONAL
4 PERSON REGARDING THE RESPONDENT'S PROGRESS WITH TREATMENT AND
5 WHETHER THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR CONVERSION OF THE
6 CIVIL COMMITMENT TO AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT,
7 CERTIFICATION FOR SHORT-TERM TREATMENT, OR SHORT-TERM
8 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, OR MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION OF
9 THE CIVIL COMMITMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 27-65-202, UNLESS CDHS
10 HAS FACILITATED A SIMILAR EXAMINATION OF THE RESPONDENT IN THE
11 PAST ONE HUNDRED TWENTY DAYS. THE RESPONDENT SHALL COOPERATE
12 WITH ANY EXAMINATIONS ORDERED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION
13 (10)(a).
14 (b) STATEMENTS MADE BY THE RESPONDENT DURING AN
15 EXAMINATION CONDUCTED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION (10) MUST NOT
16 BE USED IN ANY CRIMINAL PROSECUTION.
17 (c) GOOD CAUSE INCLUDES A RESPONDENT'S TIMELY REQUEST FOR
18 AN EVALUATION THAT ALLOWS A COMPETENCY EVALUATOR TO PROVIDE
19 A SECOND OPINION IN RESPONSE TO THE REPORT PROVIDED PURSUANT TO
20 SUBSECTION (8) OF THIS SECTION THAT OPINES THAT THE RESPONDENT
21 DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION.
22 (11) THIS SECTION IS REPEALED, EFFECTIVE JULY 1, 2031.
23 27-65-202. Termination of civil commitment - repeal.
24 (1) THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE A RESPONDENT'S CIVIL
25 COMMITMENT ORDERED PURSUANT TO SECTION 27-65-201 IF THE
26 RESPONDENT:
27 (a) NO LONGER POSES A SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS HARM TO
-144- 149

1 OTHERS; OR
2 (b) DOES NOT HAVE A MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER THAT IS LIKELY
3 TO CAUSE THE RESPONDENT TO BE A DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF
4 OR A DANGER TO OTHERS AND THE RESPONDENT HAS DEMONSTRATED
5 SUFFICIENT CAPACITY AND WILLINGNESS TO CONFORM THE RESPONDENT'S
6 CONDUCT TO THE REQUIREMENTS OF THE LAW.
7 (2) (a) IF A TREATMENT PROVIDER CHARGED WITH THE PHYSICAL
8 CARE AND CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT DETERMINES THAT THE
9 RESPONDENT MEETS THE STANDARD FOR TERMINATION FROM CIVIL
10 COMMITMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION, THE
11 PROVIDER OR THE PROVIDER'S DESIGNEE SHALL REPORT THE
12 DETERMINATION TO THE COURT THAT PLACED THE RESPONDENT INTO THE
13 PROVIDER'S CARE AND CUSTODY, THE COUNTY ATTORNEY, AND THE
14 DISTRICT ATTORNEY WHO ORIGINALLY SOUGHT CIVIL COMMITMENT OR AN
15 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-118.
16 THE COURT SHALL FURNISH A COPY OF ANY REPORTS RECEIVED TO THE
17 RESPONDENT AND THE RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL.
18 (b) THE DETERMINATION REPORTED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION
19 (2)(a) OF THIS SECTION MUST INCLUDE:
20 (I) THE TREATMENT PROVIDER'S OPINION THAT THE RESPONDENT
21 MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION OF THE CIVIL COMMITMENT
22 PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION;
23 (II) AN ASSESSMENT OF THE RESPONDENT'S RISK OF HARM TO
24 OTHERS; AND
25 (III) SPECIFIC FACTS AND EVIDENCE SUPPORTING EACH OPINION IN
26 THE REPORT, INCLUDING A SUMMARY OF THE MATERIALS REVIEWED,
27 ASSESSMENTS CONDUCTED, AND OTHER BASES OF OPINIONS RENDERED.
-145- 149

1 (3) (a) THE RESPONDENT MAY REQUEST TERMINATION OF THE CIVIL
2 COMMITMENT IN WRITING AT ANY TIME THE RESPONDENT WOULD NOT BE
3 PROHIBITED FROM HAVING A SUBSEQUENT TERMINATION TRIAL PURSUANT
4 TO SUBSECTION (7) OF THIS SECTION.
5 (b) THE COURT SHALL DENY THE REQUEST UNLESS THE REQUEST
6 FOR TERMINATION INCLUDES AN OPINION FROM A PROFESSIONAL PERSON
7 THAT THE RESPONDENT CURRENTLY MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR
8 TERMINATION OF THE CIVIL COMMITMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1)
9 OF THIS SECTION.
10 (4) (a) IF THE COURT DOES NOT DENY THE REQUEST FOR
11 TERMINATION WITHOUT A HEARING, THE COURT SHALL PERMIT THE
12 COUNTY ATTORNEY AND THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY WHO ORIGINALLY
13 SOUGHT CIVIL COMMITMENT OR ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
14 PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-118 FOURTEEN DAYS TO OBJECT TO
15 TERMINATION OR REQUEST AN OPPORTUNITY TO CONDUCT AN
16 INDEPENDENT EVALUATION BY AN EXPERT OF THE COUNTY ATTORNEY'S OR
17 DISTRICT ATTORNEY'S OWN CHOOSING AND EXPENSE.
18 (b) IF NEITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY NOR THE DISTRICT
19 ATTORNEY OBJECT OR REQUEST AN OPPORTUNITY TO REQUEST AN
20 INDEPENDENT EVALUATION, THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE THE
21 RESPONDENT'S CIVIL COMMITMENT.
22 (c) IF EITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY
23 REQUEST AN OPPORTUNITY TO CONDUCT AN INDEPENDENT EVALUATION,
24 THE COURT SHALL GRANT THAT REQUEST, ORDER THE RESPONDENT TO
25 COMPLY WITH THE EVALUATION, AND PERMIT THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR
26 DISTRICT ATTORNEY A REASONABLE PERIOD OF TIME TO IDENTIFY AN
27 EXPERT, CONDUCT THE EVALUATION, AND ISSUE A REPORT. IF BOTH THE
-146- 149

1 COUNTY ATTORNEY AND DISTRICT ATTORNEY REQUEST AN INDEPENDENT
2 EVALUATION, THE COURT SHALL TREAT THE REQUEST AS A JOINT REQUEST
3 FOR A SINGLE INDEPENDENT EVALUATION. THE COST OF THE INDEPENDENT
4 EVALUATION MUST BE PAID FOR BY THE REQUESTING PARTY.
5 (d) IF THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT COOPERATE WITH THE
6 EVALUATION, THE COURT MAY GRANT ADDITIONAL TIME OR DENY THE
7 RESPONDENT'S REQUEST FOR TERMINATION.
8 (e) UPON RECEIPT OF THE INDEPENDENT EVALUATION REPORT, THE
9 COUNTY ATTORNEY OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY MUST PROVIDE A COPY TO
10 RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL AND THE COURT WITHIN FOURTEEN DAYS.
11 (f) IF NEITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY NOR DISTRICT ATTORNEY
12 OBJECT WITHIN THE TIME PERMITTED BY THE COURT, THE COURT SHALL
13 TERMINATE THE RESPONDENT'S CIVIL COMMITMENT.
14 (5) (a) IF EITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY
15 TIMELY OBJECTS, THE COURT SHALL, AS SOON AS PRACTICABLE, BRING THE
16 RESPONDENT BEFORE THE COURT, ENSURE THE RESPONDENT AND THE
17 RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL RECEIVED A COPY OF THE REPORT, AND ADVISE
18 THE RESPONDENT OF THE RIGHT TO DEMAND A TRIAL BY THE COURT OR
19 THE RIGHT TO DEMAND, IN WRITING, A JURY TRIAL OF NOT MORE THAN SIX
20 INDIVIDUALS TO DETERMINE WHETHER THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE
21 CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
22 SECTION.
23 (b) IF THE RESPONDENT DEMANDS A TRIAL BEFORE THE COURT, THE
24 COURT SHALL SCHEDULE THE TRIAL TO BEGIN WITHIN THIRTY-FIVE DAYS
25 AFTER THE DEMAND. IF THE RESPONDENT DEMANDS A JURY TRIAL, THE
26 COURT SHALL SCHEDULE THE TRIAL TO BEGIN WITHIN SEVENTY-TWO DAYS
27 AFTER THE DEMAND. A DELAY ATTRIBUTABLE TO THE RESPONDENT IS
-147- 149

1 EXCLUDED FROM THE TIME LIMITATIONS IN THIS SUBSECTION (5)(b). IF THE
2 COURT DOES NOT BEGIN THE TRIAL WITHIN THE TIME PERMITTED
3 PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION (5)(b), THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE THE
4 CIVIL COMMITMENT AND RELEASE THE RESPONDENT FROM THE PROVIDER'S
5 CARE AND CUSTODY.
6 (c) IF THE COUNTY ATTORNEY DOES NOT OBJECT TO TERMINATION
7 BUT THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY DOES, THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY HAS
8 STANDING TO SERVE AS THE COUNTY ATTORNEY FOR THE LIMITED PURPOSE
9 OF TRIAL AND ANY APPEALS RELATED TO THE TRIAL. THE COUNTY
10 ATTORNEY SHALL TIMELY PROVIDE THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY ALL
11 INFORMATION AND RECORDS RELEVANT TO THE TRIAL IN THE COUNTY
12 ATTORNEY'S POSSESSION OR CONTROL WITHOUT SUBPOENA OR COURT
13 ORDER. THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY'S LIMITED STANDING DOES NOT EXTEND
14 TO ANY OTHER MATTERS RELATED TO THE CIVIL COMMITMENT AND
15 TERMINATES UPON RESOLUTION OF THE TRIAL AND ANY RELATED APPEALS.
16 (6) (a) AT THE TRIAL, IF ANY EVIDENCE IS INTRODUCED THAT
17 SHOWS THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION
18 OF THE CIVIL COMMITMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
19 SECTION, THE RESPONDENT HAS THE BURDEN TO SHOW BY A
20 PREPONDERANCE OF THE EVIDENCE THAT THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE
21 CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
22 SECTION.
23 (b) IF THE TRIER OF FACT FINDS, BY A PREPONDERANCE OF THE
24 EVIDENCE, THAT THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION
25 PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION, THE COURT SHALL ORDER
26 THE RESPONDENT RELEASED FROM THE PROVIDER'S CARE AND CUSTODY
27 AND TERMINATE THE RESPONDENT'S CIVIL COMMITMENT. IF THE TRIER OF
-148- 149

1 FACT FINDS, BY A PREPONDERANCE OF THE EVIDENCE, THAT THE
2 RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT
3 TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION, THE COURT SHALL CONTINUE THE
4 CIVIL COMMITMENT AND MAY ENTER OR MODIFY ANY ORDERS TO ASSIST
5 IN PROGRESSING THE TREATMENT OF THE RESPONDENT OR THAT ARE
6 NECESSARY TO PROTECT THE PUBLIC.
7 (7) IF THE TRIER OF FACT FINDS THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET
8 THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
9 SECTION, THE RESPONDENT IS NOT ENTITLED TO ANOTHER TERMINATION
10 TRIAL WITHIN ONE YEAR AFTER THE CONCLUSION OF THE PREVIOUS TRIAL
11 FOR TERMINATION.
12 (8) IF THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR
13 TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION AND HAS A
14 MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER THAT IS AN INTELLECTUAL AND
15 DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR A NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER, AS THOSE
16 TERMS ARE DEFINED IN SECTION 25.5-10-501, WITHOUT HAVING ANY
17 OTHER MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER THAT IS NOT AN INTELLECTUAL AND
18 DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER AND THAT
19 SUBSTANTIALLY CONTRIBUTES TO WHETHER THE RESPONDENT IS A
20 DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR A DANGER TO OTHERS, OR IS
21 GRAVELY DISABLED, THE COURT SHALL CONVERT THE CIVIL COMMITMENT
22 TO AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND MAY MODIFY THE TERMS
23 OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IN ACCORDANCE WITH
24 SECTION 25.5-10-507.
25 (9) IF THE DEFENDANT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR
26 TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION AND HAS
27 CO-OCCURRING MENTAL HEALTH DISORDERS THAT INCLUDE AN
-149- 149

1 INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR A NEUROCOGNITIVE
2 DISORDER, AS THOSE TERMS ARE DEFINED IN SECTION 25.5-10-501, THE
3 COURT MAY, UPON THE RECOMMENDATION OF CDHS, CONVERT A CIVIL
4 COMMITMENT TO AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND MODIFY
5 THE TERMS OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IN ACCORDANCE
6 WITH SECTION 25.5-10-507.
7 (10) THIS SECTION IS REPEALED, EFFECTIVE JULY 1, 2031.
8 SECTION 22. In Colorado Revised Statutes, add part 5 to article
9 10 of title 25.5 as follows:
10 PART 5
11 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND
12 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
13 25.5-10-501. Definitions.
14 AS USED IN THIS PART 5, UNLESS THE CONTEXT OTHERWISE
15 REQUIRES:
16 (1) "BEHAVIORAL HEALTH ADMINISTRATION" OR "BHA" MEANS
17 THE BEHAVIORAL HEALTH ADMINISTRATION ESTABLISHED IN SECTION
18 27-60-203.
19 (2) "DANGER TO OTHERS" HAS THE MEANING SET FORTH IN
20 SECTION 27-65-102.
21 (3) "DANGER TO THE PERSON'S SELF", OR SIMILAR TERMINOLOGY,
22 HAS THE MEANING SET FORTH IN SECTION 27-65-102.
23 (4) "DEPARTMENT OF HEALTH CARE POLICY AND FINANCING" OR
24 "HCPF" MEANS THE DEPARTMENT OF HEALTH CARE POLICY AND
25 FINANCING CREATED IN SECTION 24-1-119.5.
26 (5) "DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES" OR "CDHS" MEANS THE
27 DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES.
-150- 149

1 (6) "INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY" HAS THE
2 MEANING SET FORTH IN SECTION 25.5-10-202.
3 (7) "INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL" HAS THE MEANING SET FORTH
4 IN SECTION 27-65-102.
5 (8) "MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER" HAS THE MEANING SET FORTH IN
6 SECTION 27-65-102.
7 (9) "NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER" MEANS A SUBSTANTIAL AND
8 PERSISTENT ACQUIRED DISORDER OF THE COGNITIVE OR NEUROLOGICAL
9 PROCESSES THAT GROSSLY IMPAIRS JUDGMENT, MEMORY, OR CAPACITY TO
10 RECOGNIZE REALITY OR TO CONTROL BEHAVIOR, GENERAL INTELLECTUAL
11 FUNCTIONING, OR ADAPTIVE BEHAVIOR THAT IS ATTRIBUTABLE TO A
12 NEUROLOGICAL OR COGNITIVE DISORDER OR RELATED CONDITION,
13 INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, A TRAUMATIC BRAIN INJURY, A
14 DEGENERATIVE DISORDER, OR DEMENTIA. "NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER"
15 DOES NOT INCLUDE AN INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY.
16 (10) "PROFESSIONAL PERSON" HAS THE MEANING SET FORTH IN
17 SECTION 27-65-102.
18 (11) "REGIONAL CENTER" MEANS A FACILITY OR PROGRAM
19 OPERATED DIRECTLY BY THE DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES THAT
20 PROVIDES SERVICES AND SUPPORTS TO PERSONS WITH INTELLECTUAL AND
21 DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITIES.
22 (12) "SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS HARM TO OTHERS" HAS THE
23 MEANING SET FORTH IN SECTION 27-65-102.
24 25.5-10-502. Criteria and standards for protective placement.
25 (1) THE COURT MAY ORDER THE PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OF A
26 RESPONDENT IF:
27 (a) THE RESPONDENT, BY CLEAR AND CONVINCING EVIDENCE, HAS
-151- 149

1 A NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER;
2 (b) WITH THE CONSIDERATION OF ALL REASONABLY AVAILABLE
3 INFORMATION, INCLUDING THE RELEVANT HISTORY OF THE RESPONDENT,
4 THERE ARE REASONABLE GROUNDS TO BELIEVE THAT THE RESPONDENT
5 WILL NOT REMAIN IN A VOLUNTARY TREATMENT PROGRAM; AND
6 (c) THE RESPONDENT, BY CLEAR AND CONVINCING EVIDENCE, IS A
7 DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR A DANGER TO OTHERS.
8 (2) WHEN EVALUATING A PERSON TO DETERMINE WHETHER THE
9 PERSON MEETS THE CRITERIA PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
10 SECTION, THE COURT, EVALUATOR, INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL, OR
11 PROFESSIONAL PERSON SHALL TAKE INTO CONSIDERATION:
12 (a) THE PERSON'S STATEMENTS AND INSIGHTS INTO THE PERSON'S
13 OWN NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER;
14 (b) CLINICAL DIAGNOSIS AND CLINICAL PERSPECTIVE ON THE
15 PERSON'S CURRENT MENTAL STATE AND PROGNOSIS;
16 (c) THE PERSON'S WILLINGNESS TO VOLUNTARILY SEEK AND
17 COMPLY WITH A TREATMENT PLAN IN THE REASONABLY FORESEEABLE
18 FUTURE;
19 (d) RECENT OVERT ACTS BY THE PERSON TO THREATEN, CAUSE, OR
20 ATTEMPT TO CAUSE HARM TO THE PERSON'S SELF OR OTHERS;
21 (e) PREVIOUS PATTERNS OF DETERIORATION THAT RESULTED IN
22 THE PERSON'S HOSPITALIZATION, ARREST, CERTIFICATION FOR
23 SHORT-TERM TREATMENT, OR PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT;
24 (f) WHETHER THE PERSON WAS FOUND IN A CONDITION WHERE THE
25 PERSON WAS NOT ABLE TO CARE FOR THE PERSON'S OWN BASIC NEEDS IN
26 ORDER TO AVOID THE RISK OF SERIOUS PHYSICAL HARM; AND
27 (g) THE FREQUENCY, RECENCY, AND SEVERITY OF THE
-152- 149

1 CONSIDERATIONS DESCRIBED IN SUBSECTIONS (2)(b) TO (2)(f) OF THIS
2 SECTION AND THE LIKELIHOOD THAT THE CONDITIONS AND EVENTS WILL
3 REOCCUR WITHOUT INVOLUNTARY TREATMENT.
4 (3) WHEN EVALUATING WHETHER A PERSON IS A DANGER TO THE
5 PERSON'S SELF OR A DANGER TO OTHERS, IS GRAVELY DISABLED, OR POSES
6 A SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS HARM TO OTHERS, THE COURT,
7 EVALUATOR, OR INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL SHALL, WHENEVER
8 POSSIBLE, USE ALL REASONABLE EFFORTS TO LEARN ABOUT PRIOR
9 RELEVANT BEHAVIORS AND PRIOR DIAGNOSES THROUGH AVAILABLE AND
10 RELIABLE SOURCES, INCLUDING THE PERSON'S PRIOR MEDICAL AND
11 MENTAL HEALTH RECORDS, POLICE REPORTS, AND INFORMATION FROM
12 RELIABLE INDIVIDUALS WHO HAVE A RELATIONSHIP OR REGULAR
13 SUBSTANTIAL INTERACTIONS WITH THE PERSON.
14 (4) THE COURT OR PROFESSIONAL PERSON SHALL ASSESS AN
15 INCARCERATED PERSON AS IF THE PERSON WERE IN THE COMMUNITY WHEN
16 EVALUATING WHETHER THE PERSON MEETS THE CRITERIA PURSUANT TO
17 SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION.
18
19 25.5-10-503. Short-term protective placement for incompetent
20 defendants in a criminal matter.
21 (1) UPON PETITION OF A PROFESSIONAL PERSON OR INTERVENING
22 PROFESSIONAL ACTING WITHIN THE SCOPE OF THEIR AUTHORITY AND
23 LICENSURE, AN APPOINTED LEGAL GUARDIAN, OR A REPRESENTATIVE OF
24 THE BHA OR HCPF, A COURT MAY CERTIFY A PERSON FOR SHORT-TERM
25 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT FOR NOT MORE THAN THREE MONTHS UNDER THE
26 FOLLOWING CONDITIONS:
27 (a) THE PERSON IS OR RECENTLY WAS A DEFENDANT IN A CRIMINAL
-153- 149

1 MATTER IN WHICH THE PERSON HAS BEEN FOUND INCOMPETENT TO
2 PROCEED;
3 (b) THE COURT HEARING THE CRIMINAL MATTER REFERRED THE
4 MATTER FOR FILING OF A PETITION PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-117;
5 (c) A PROFESSIONAL PERSON OR INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL
6 ACTING WITHIN THE SCOPE OF THEIR AUTHORITY AND LICENSURE:
7 (I) HAS AN ESTABLISHED TREATMENT RELATIONSHIP WITH THE
8 PERSON, INCLUDING HAVING PROVIDED CARE TO THE PERSON IN THE PAST
9 THREE MONTHS, WHICH CARE FORMS THE BASIS FOR REQUESTING THE
10 SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT; AND
11 (II) HAS EVALUATED THE PERSON WITHIN THE PAST THREE
12 MONTHS AND PRODUCED A WRITTEN OPINION THAT THE PERSON MEETS
13 THE CRITERIA FOR PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
14 25.5-10-502;
15 (d) THERE IS A SKILLED NURSING FACILITY, A REGIONAL CENTER,
16 OR ANOTHER PLACEMENT WILLING TO ACCEPT CARE AND CUSTODY OF THE
17 RESPONDENT AND TO HOLD THE PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT; AND
18 (e) THE PERSON, THE PERSON'S LEGAL GUARDIAN, AND THE
19 PERSON'S LAY PERSON, IF APPLICABLE, HAVE BEEN ADVISED OF THE
20 PERSON'S RIGHT TO AN ATTORNEY AND TO CONTEST THE SHORT-TERM
21 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
22 (2) THE PETITION FILED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
23 SECTION MUST:
24 (a) STATE SUFFICIENT FACTS TO ESTABLISH REASONABLE GROUNDS
25 THAT THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR SHORT-TERM
26 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-502, INCLUDING
27 ATTACHING THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON'S OR INTERVENING
-154- 149

1 PROFESSIONAL'S WRITTEN OPINION PRODUCED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION
2 (1)(c)(II) OF THIS SECTION;
3 (b) BE FILED WITHIN FOURTEEN DAYS AFTER THE INITIATING PARTY
4 RECEIVED THE COURT ORDER FROM THE CRIMINAL COURT INITIATING THE
5 SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT; AND
6 (c) BE FILED WITH THE COURT IN THE COUNTY WHERE THE
7 RESPONDENT RESIDED OR WAS PHYSICALLY PRESENT IMMEDIATELY PRIOR
8 TO THE FILING OF THE PETITION; EXCEPT THAT, IF THE PERSON WAS
9 ARRESTED FOR THE PRIOR CASE AND HELD IN CUSTODY, THE PETITION MAY
10 BE FILED IN THE COUNTY WHERE THE RESPONDENT RESIDED OR WAS
11 PHYSICALLY PRESENT IMMEDIATELY PRIOR TO THE RESPONDENT'S ARREST.
12 (3) WITHIN TWENTY-FOUR HOURS AFTER CERTIFICATION, COPIES
13 OF THE SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT MUST BE PERSONALLY
14 DELIVERED TO THE RESPONDENT AND HCPF WHO SHALL RETAIN A COPY
15 OF THE CERTIFICATION AS PART OF THE RESPONDENT'S RECORD. IF THE
16 CRIMINAL CASE IS PENDING, OR NOT YET DISMISSED, THE PETITIONING
17 PARTY SHALL PROVIDE NOTICE OF THE FILING OF THE PETITION TO THE
18 CRIMINAL COURT. THE CRIMINAL COURT SHALL PROVIDE THE NOTICE TO
19 THE PROSECUTING AND DEFENSE ATTORNEYS IN THE CRIMINAL CASE AND
20 ANY ATTORNEY APPOINTED THE RESPONDENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION
21 (5) OF THIS SECTION.
22 (4) THE PETITIONER SHALL ASK THE RESPONDENT TO DESIGNATE
23 ONE OTHER PERSON WHOM THE RESPONDENT WANTS TO BE INFORMED
24 REGARDING THE PETITION. IF THE RESPONDENT IS INCAPABLE OF MAKING
25 A DESIGNATION AT THE TIME THE PETITION IS DELIVERED, THE COURT MAY
26 ASK THE RESPONDENT TO DESIGNATE A PERSON AS SOON AS THE
27 RESPONDENT IS CAPABLE. IF THE PETITIONER FAILS TO ASK THE
-155- 149

1 RESPONDENT TO DESIGNATE A PERSON, THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY
2 APPOINTED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (5) OF THIS SECTION SHALL REPORT
3 TO THE COURT ANY PERSON WHOM THE RESPONDENT WANTS TO BE
4 INFORMED REGARDING THE PETITION.
5 (5) WHENEVER A PETITION IS FILED PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION,
6 THE COURT SHALL IMMEDIATELY APPOINT AN ATTORNEY TO REPRESENT
7 THE RESPONDENT. THE COURT SHALL PROVIDE THE RESPONDENT WITH A
8 WRITTEN NOTICE THAT THE RESPONDENT HAS A RIGHT TO A HEARING ON
9 THE PETITION AND MAY MAKE A WRITTEN REQUEST FOR A JURY TRIAL. THE
10 RESPONDENT HAS THE RIGHT TO AN ATTORNEY FOR ALL PROCEEDINGS
11 CONDUCTED PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION, INCLUDING ANY APPEALS. THE
12 ATTORNEY REPRESENTING THE RESPONDENT MUST BE PROVIDED WITH A
13 COPY OF THE PETITION AND ANY SUPPORTING MATERIALS IMMEDIATELY
14 UPON THE ATTORNEY'S APPOINTMENT. THE RESPONDENT MAY ONLY WAIVE
15 COUNSEL WHEN THE RESPONDENT MAKES A KNOWING AND VOLUNTARY
16 WAIVER IN FRONT OF THE COURT.
17 (6) UPON FILING A PETITION PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION AND
18 AFFORDING THE RESPONDENT A CHANCE TO CONTEST THE PETITION, THE
19 COURT MAY GRANT OR DENY THE PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT BASED ON THE
20 FACTS ESTABLISHED IN THE PETITION, SUBJECT TO THE COURT'S FURTHER
21 REVIEW OR A JURY TRIAL.
22 (7) WITHIN FOURTEEN DAYS AFTER RECEIPT OF THE PETITION FILED
23 PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION, THE RESPONDENT, OR THE RESPONDENT'S
24 ATTORNEY, MAY REQUEST A JURY TRIAL BY FILING A WRITTEN MOTION
25 WITH THE COURT.
26 (8) THE RESPONDENT MAY KNOWINGLY AND VOLUNTARILY
27 CONSENT TO THE PETITION IN WRITING.
-156- 149

1 (9) THE RESPONDENT OR THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY MAY, AT
2 ANY TIME, FILE A WRITTEN REQUEST FOR THE COURT TO REVIEW THE
3 SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT. IF A REVIEW IS REQUESTED, THE
4 COURT SHALL HEAR THE MATTER WITHIN FOURTEEN DAYS AFTER THE
5 REQUEST, AND THE COURT SHALL GIVE NOTICE TO THE RESPONDENT, THE
6 RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY, HCPF, AND THE COMMUNITY OR FACILITY
7 PROVIDER WHO IS OR WILL PROVIDE TREATMENT. THE HEARING MUST BE
8 HELD IN ACCORDANCE WITH SECTION 25.5-10-510. AT THE CONCLUSION
9 OF THE HEARING, THE COURT MAY ENTER OR CONFIRM THE SHORT-TERM
10 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, DISCHARGE THE RESPONDENT, OR ENTER ANY
11 OTHER APPROPRIATE ORDER.
12 (10) (a) THE BHA, HCPF, THE DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES,
13 AND CARE PROVIDERS MAY SHARE INFORMATION WITH EACH OTHER AND
14 THE PARTIES AS NECESSARY. THE BHA, HCPF, THE DEPARTMENT OF
15 HUMAN SERVICES, AND CARE PROVIDERS MAY RECEIVE AND POSSESS ALL
16 INFORMATION RELEVANT TO THE PROCEEDINGS HELD PURSUANT TO THIS
17 SECTION, INCLUDING ANY EVALUATIONS; ANY MEDICAL AND MENTAL
18 HEALTH RECORDS FOR WHICH A WAIVER OR PRIVILEGE HAS BEEN FOUND
19 IN PROCEEDINGS HELD PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5, ARTICLE 65 OF TITLE 27,
20 OR ARTICLE 8 OR 8.5 OF TITLE 16; AND RELEVANT CRIMINAL JUSTICE
21 RECORDS, INCLUDING ANY CRIMINAL HISTORY OF THE RESPONDENT.
22 (b) THE COURT MAY ORDER THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY RESPONSIBLE
23 FOR PROSECUTING A CRIMINAL CASE THAT LED TO PROCEEDINGS
24 PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION OR SECTION 16-8.5-117 TO SEND RELEVANT
25 RECORDS TO HCPF WITHIN SEVEN DAYS AFTER THE COURT'S ORDER.
26 (c) ANY CURRENT OR FORMER ATTORNEY WHO REPRESENTED THE
27 RESPONDENT IN ANY PROCEEDING SHALL SEND MATERIALS TO HCPF WITH
-157- 149

1 THE RESPONDENT'S CONSENT.
2 (11) (a) UNLESS AN APPROPRIATE PROVIDER HAS ALREADY BEEN
3 IDENTIFIED AND IS WILLING TO ACCEPT THE PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, THE
4 COURT SHALL NOTIFY HCPF REGARDING THE NEED FOR CARE
5 COORDINATION.
6 (b) ONCE HCPF IS NOTIFIED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (11)(a) OF
7 THIS SECTION, HCPF SHALL MAKE DILIGENT EFFORTS TO FIND A PROVIDER
8 FOR THE RESPONDENT, ASSIST WITH INTENSIVE COORDINATION, AND
9 MITIGATE BARRIERS TO APPROPRIATE PLACEMENT. HCPF SHALL
10 COLLABORATE AND COORDINATE WITH OTHER STATE EXECUTIVE
11 AGENCIES, LAW ENFORCEMENT, THE COURT, THE PARTIES, COMMUNITY
12 PARTNERS, REGIONAL ACCOUNTABLE ENTITIES, AND CASE MANAGEMENT
13 AGENCIES TO IDENTIFY AN APPROPRIATE PLACEMENT FOR THE
14 RESPONDENT, WHICH PLACEMENT IS SUBJECT TO MEDICAID
15 REIMBURSEMENT.
16 (c) THE CASE MANAGEMENT AGENCY MUST PROVIDE CASE
17 MANAGEMENT SERVICES, AS DEFINED IN SECTION 25.5-6-1702.
18 (d) HCPF SHALL KEEP THE COURT INFORMED, IN WRITING, OF
19 EFFORTS MADE TO FIND AN APPROPRIATE PLACEMENT FOR THE
20 RESPONDENT.
21 (12) UPON ORDERING A SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OF
22 THE RESPONDENT, THE PROVIDER ORDERED TO RECEIVE THE RESPONDENT
23 HAS CARE AND PHYSICAL CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT.
24 (13) WHENEVER IT APPEARS TO THE COURT THAT A RESPONDENT
25 IN A SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT SHOULD BE TRANSFERRED TO
26 ANOTHER PROVIDER FOR TREATMENT AND THE SAFETY OF THE
27 RESPONDENT OR THE PUBLIC REQUIRES THAT THE RESPONDENT BE
-158- 149

1 TRANSPORTED BY A SECURE TRANSPORTATION PROVIDER OR A LAW
2 ENFORCEMENT AGENCY, THE COURT MAY ISSUE AN ORDER DIRECTING THE
3 LAW ENFORCEMENT AGENCY WHERE THE RESPONDENT RESIDES OR SECURE
4 TRANSPORTATION PROVIDER TO DELIVER THE RESPONDENT TO THE
5 DESIGNATED PROVIDER.
6 (14) IN ACCORDANCE WITH THE PROCEDURES DESCRIBED IN
7 SECTION 25.5-10-506, A SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT MAY BE
8 TERMINATED UPON THE SIGNATURE OF THE TREATING MEDICAL
9 PROFESSIONAL AND THE MEDICAL DIRECTOR OF THE FACILITY. A FACILITY
10 OR PROGRAM SHALL MAKE THE RESPONDENT'S DISCHARGE INSTRUCTIONS
11 AVAILABLE TO THE RESPONDENT, THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY, AND THE
12 RESPONDENT'S LEGAL GUARDIAN.
13 (15) IF THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON IN CHARGE OF THE
14 RESPONDENT'S EVALUATION AND TREATMENT BELIEVES THAT A PERIOD
15 LONGER THAN THREE MONTHS IS NECESSARY TO TREAT THE RESPONDENT,
16 THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON SHALL FILE WITH THE COURT A REQUEST FOR
17 AN EXTENDED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AT LEAST THIRTY DAYS PRIOR TO
18 THE EXPIRATION DATE OF THE ORIGINAL PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT. AN
19 EXTENDED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT FOR TREATMENT MUST NOT BE FOR A
20 PERIOD OF MORE THAN THREE MONTHS. THE RESPONDENT IS ENTITLED TO
21 A HEARING ON THE EXTENDED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT UNDER THE SAME
22 CONDITIONS AS AN ORIGINAL PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT. THE ATTORNEY
23 INITIALLY REPRESENTING THE RESPONDENT SHALL CONTINUE TO
24 REPRESENT THE RESPONDENT, UNLESS THE COURT APPOINTS ANOTHER
25 ATTORNEY.
26 25.5-10-504. Long-term protective placement of persons with
27 a neurocognitive disorder - procedure.
-159- 149

1 (1) IF A RESPONDENT HAS RECEIVED AN EXTENDED PROTECTIVE
2 PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-503, THE PROFESSIONAL
3 PERSON IN CHARGE OF THE EXTENDED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, THE
4 APPOINTED LEGAL GUARDIAN, OR THE COUNTY ATTORNEY MAY FILE A
5 PETITION WITH THE COURT AT LEAST THIRTY DAYS PRIOR TO THE
6 EXPIRATION DATE OF THE EXTENDED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT FOR A
7 LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OF THE RESPONDENT UNDER THE
8 FOLLOWING CONDITIONS:
9 (a) THE PROFESSIONAL STAFF OF THE AGENCY OR FACILITY
10 PROVIDING SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT HAS ANALYZED THE
11 RESPONDENT'S CONDITION AND FOUND THE RESPONDENT CONTINUES TO
12 MEET THE CRITERIA FOR PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
13 25.5-10-502 (1); AND
14 (b) HCPF, IN COLLABORATION WITH THE BHA, HAS IDENTIFIED AN
15 APPROPRIATE PROVIDER OR PLACEMENT TO PROVIDE CARE AND
16 TREATMENT OF THE RESPONDENT.
17 (2) A PETITION FOR LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT MUST
18 INCLUDE A REQUEST FOR A HEARING BEFORE THE COURT PRIOR TO THE
19 EXPIRATION OF SIX MONTHS AFTER THE DATE OF THE ORIGINAL ORDER FOR
20 A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND PROVIDE A RECOMMENDATION AS TO
21 WHETHER THE LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT SHOULD TAKE PLACE
22 ON AN INPATIENT OR OUTPATIENT BASIS. A COPY OF THE PETITION MUST
23 BE DELIVERED PERSONALLY TO THE RESPONDENT FOR WHOM LONG-TERM
24 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS SOUGHT AND ELECTRONICALLY DELIVERED TO
25 THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY OF RECORD SIMULTANEOUSLY WITH THE
26 FILING.
27 (3) WITHIN TEN DAYS AFTER RECEIPT OF THE PETITION, THE
-160- 149

1 RESPONDENT MAY REQUEST A HEARING BEFORE THE COURT OR A JURY
2 TRIAL BY FILING A WRITTEN REQUEST WITH THE COURT.
3 (4) THE COURT OR JURY SHALL DETERMINE WHETHER THE
4 CONDITIONS OF SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION ARE MET AND WHETHER
5 THE RESPONDENT CONTINUES TO MEET THE CRITERIA AND STANDARDS FOR
6 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-502 (1). THE
7 COURT SHALL ISSUE AN ORDER OF LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
8 FOR A TERM NOT TO EXCEED SIX MONTHS, DISCHARGE THE RESPONDENT
9 FOR WHOM LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT WAS SOUGHT, OR ENTER
10 ANY OTHER APPROPRIATE ORDER. AN ORDER FOR LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE
11 PLACEMENT MUST GRANT CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT TO THE
12 PROVIDER OR PLACEMENT IDENTIFIED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1)(b) OF
13 THIS SECTION. WHEN A PETITION CONTAINS A REQUEST THAT A SPECIFIC
14 LEGAL DISABILITY BE IMPOSED OR THAT A SPECIFIC LEGAL RIGHT BE
15 DEPRIVED, THE COURT MAY ORDER THE DISABILITY IMPOSED OR THE RIGHT
16 DEPRIVED IF THE COURT OR A JURY HAS DETERMINED THAT THE
17 RESPONDENT MEETS THE CRITERIA AND STANDARDS FOR PROTECTIVE
18 PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-502 (1) AND THAT, AS A
19 RESULT, THE RESPONDENT IS UNABLE TO COMPETENTLY EXERCISE THE
20 SPECIFIC LEGAL RIGHT OR PERFORM THE FUNCTION FOR WHICH THE
21 DISABILITY IS SOUGHT TO BE IMPOSED. ANY INTERESTED PERSON MAY ASK
22 LEAVE OF THE COURT TO INTERVENE AS A COPETITIONER FOR THE PURPOSE
23 OF SEEKING THE IMPOSITION OF A LEGAL DISABILITY OR THE DEPRIVATION
24 OF A LEGAL RIGHT.
25 (5) AN ORIGINAL ORDER OF LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
26 AND AN EXTENSION OF THE ORDER EXPIRES ON THE DATE SPECIFIED,
27 UNLESS FURTHER EXTENDED AS PROVIDED IN THIS SUBSECTION (5). IF AN
-161- 149

1 EXTENSION IS BEING SOUGHT, THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON IN CHARGE OF
2 THE EVALUATION AND TREATMENT SHALL CERTIFY TO THE COURT AT
3 LEAST THIRTY DAYS PRIOR TO THE EXPIRATION DATE OF THE ORDER IN
4 FORCE THAT AN EXTENSION OF THE ORDER IS NECESSARY FOR THE
5 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OF THE RESPONDENT SUBJECT TO THE ORDER IN
6 FORCE, AND A COPY OF THE ORDER MUST BE SIMULTANEOUSLY DELIVERED
7 TO THE RESPONDENT AND ELECTRONICALLY DELIVERED TO THE
8 RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY OF RECORD. AT LEAST TWENTY DAYS BEFORE
9 THE EXPIRATION OF THE ORDER, THE COURT SHALL GIVE WRITTEN NOTICE
10 TO THE RESPONDENT AND THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY OF RECORD THAT
11 A HEARING UPON THE EXTENSION MAY BE HAD BEFORE THE COURT OR A
12 JURY UPON WRITTEN REQUEST TO THE COURT WITHIN TEN DAYS AFTER
13 RECEIPT OF THE NOTICE. IF A HEARING IS NOT TIMELY REQUESTED BY THE
14 RESPONDENT, THE COURT MAY PROCEED EX PARTE. IF A HEARING IS
15 TIMELY REQUESTED, THE HEARING MUST BE HELD BEFORE THE EXPIRATION
16 DATE OF THE ORDER IN FORCE. IF THE COURT OR JURY FINDS THAT THE
17 CONDITIONS OF SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION CONTINUE TO BE MET
18 AND THAT THE RESPONDENT CONTINUES TO MEET THE CRITERIA AND
19 STANDARDS FOR PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
20 25.5-10-502 (1), THE COURT SHALL ISSUE AN EXTENSION OF THE ORDER.
21 ANY EXTENSION MUST NOT EXCEED SIX MONTHS, BUT THERE MAY BE AS
22 MANY EXTENSIONS AS THE COURT ORDERS PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION.
23 (6) A RESPONDENT PLACED IN LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE
24 PLACEMENT MAY BE DISCHARGED FROM THE PROVIDER OR FACILITY UPON
25 THE SIGNATURE OF THE TREATING PROFESSIONAL PERSON AND MEDICAL
26 DIRECTOR OF THE FACILITY, AND THE FACILITY SHALL NOTIFY THE COURT
27 PRIOR TO THE RESPONDENT'S DISCHARGE. THE FACILITY SHALL MAKE
-162- 149

1 THE RESPONDENT'S DISCHARGE INSTRUCTIONS AVAILABLE TO THE
2 RESPONDENT, THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY, THE RESPONDENT'S LAY
3 PERSON, AND THE RESPONDENT'S LEGAL GUARDIAN, IF APPLICABLE, WITHIN
4 ONE WEEK AFTER DISCHARGE, IF REQUESTED. A FACILITY THAT IS
5 TRANSFERRING A RESPONDENT TO A DIFFERENT FACILITY OR TO AN
6 OUTPATIENT PROGRAM SHALL PROVIDE ALL TREATMENT RECORDS TO THE
7 FACILITY OR PROVIDER ACCEPTING THE RESPONDENT AT LEAST
8 TWENTY-FOUR HOURS PRIOR TO THE TRANSFER.
9
10 25.5-10-505. Connect respondent to home- and
11 community-based services.
12 WHEN A RESPONDENT IS DISCHARGED FROM A PROTECTIVE
13 PLACEMENT OR A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS TERMINATED, HCPF SHALL
14 REFER THE RESPONDENT TO ANY HOME- AND COMMUNITY-BASED SERVICES
15 FOR WHICH THE RESPONDENT MAY BE ELIGIBLE AND SHALL MAKE DILIGENT
16 EFFORTS TO CONNECT THE RESPONDENT WITH HOME- AND
17 COMMUNITY-BASED SERVICES.
18 25.5-10-506. Termination of protective placement - short-term
19 and long-term placement.
20 (1) A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT TERMINATES WHEN THE
21 PROFESSIONAL PERSON IN CHARGE OF TREATMENT OF THE RESPONDENT,
22 AFTER A REASONABLE OBSERVATION AND TREATMENT PERIOD,
23 DETERMINES THE RESPONDENT NO LONGER MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR
24 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-502 (1).
25 (2) THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON IN CHARGE OF THE
26 RESPONDENT'S CARE SHALL NOT APPROVE THE TERMINATION OF THE
27 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT UNLESS TWO PROFESSIONAL PERSONS
-163- 149

1 INDEPENDENTLY EVALUATE THE RESPONDENT AND INDEPENDENTLY OPINE
2 THAT THE RESPONDENT NO LONGER MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR PROTECTIVE
3 PLACEMENT TREATMENT. ONE OF THE OPINIONS MUST BE FROM THE
4 PROFESSIONAL PERSON WHO IS MOST RESPONSIBLE FOR INTERACTING WITH
5 AND PROVIDING DIRECT CARE AND TREATMENT TO THE RESPONDENT. THIS
6 REQUIREMENT DOES NOT APPLY IF A PROVIDER EMPLOYS AND CONTRACTS
7 WITH ONLY ONE PROFESSIONAL PERSON.
8 25.5-10-507. Court supervision of incompetent and
9 unrestorable persons ordered into an enhanced protective placement.
10 (1) ONCE A CRIMINAL COURT HAS TRANSFERRED JURISDICTION OF
11 AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-118
12 TO A CIVIL COURT WITH JURISDICTION PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-509,
13 THE CIVIL COURT HAS EXCLUSIVE JURISDICTION OVER THE ENHANCED
14 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
15 (2) UPON RECEIVING JURISDICTION OF AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
16 PLACEMENT, THE COURT SHALL:
17 (a) NOTIFY THE COUNTY ATTORNEY;
18 (b) APPOINT AN ATTORNEY TO REPRESENT THE RESPONDENT AND
19 PROVIDE COPIES OF ANY DOCUMENTS SENT FROM THE CRIMINAL COURT TO
20 THE RESPONDENT'S ATTORNEY; AND
21 (c) SET A REVIEW HEARING AND ORDER THE RESPONDENT
22 BROUGHT BEFORE THE COURT.
23 (3) AT THE REVIEW HEARING, THE COURT SHALL:
24 (a) ENSURE THE RESPONDENT IS REPRESENTED BY COUNSEL; AND
25 (b) ADVISE THE RESPONDENT OF THE FOLLOWING RIGHTS:
26 (I) THE RIGHT TO APPEAR IN PERSON AT ANY PROCEEDING, UNLESS
27 WAIVED BY THE RESPONDENT;
-164- 149

1 (II) THE RIGHT TO BE REPRESENTED BY PRIVATELY RETAINED
2 COUNSEL, OR COURT-APPOINTED COUNSEL IF THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT
3 HAVE PRIVATELY RETAINED COUNSEL, DURING ANY PROCEEDING HELD
4 PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5, INCLUDING ANY APPEALS;
5 (III) THE RIGHT TO REQUEST MODIFICATION OF THE TERMS OF THE
6 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT; AND
7 (IV) THE RIGHT TO PERIODIC REVIEW OF THE ENHANCED
8 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND THE RIGHT TO CONTEST, INCLUDING BY
9 TRIAL, WHETHER THE RESPONDENT QUALIFIES FOR TERMINATION OF THE
10 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
11 (4) AT ANY TIME DURING THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT,
12 THE COURT MAY:
13 (a) MODIFY ANY COURT ORDER OR ANY TERM OF THE ENHANCED
14 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT UPON REQUEST OF THE PARTIES AFTER GIVING
15 THE PARTIES AN OPPORTUNITY TO OBJECT AND BE HEARD;
16 (b) ISSUE A WARRANT FOR THE RESPONDENT'S ARREST AND ORDER
17 THE RESPONDENT BE BROUGHT BEFORE THE COURT IF THE COURT
18 DETERMINES THE RESPONDENT HAS FAILED TO COMPLY WITH ANY OF THE
19 COURT'S ORDERS, INCLUDING THE RESPONDENT'S UNAUTHORIZED
20 DEPARTURE FROM THE PHYSICAL CUSTODY OF A PROVIDER;
21 (c) ORDER CDHS TO PROVIDE TO THE COURT:
22 (I) AN UPDATED OPINION ON WHETHER THE RESPONDENT MEETS
23 THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
24 PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-508; AND
25 (II) AN OPINION ON WHETHER THE RESPONDENT HAS AN
26 INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR NEUROCOGNITIVE
27 DISORDER THAT SUBSTANTIALLY CONTRIBUTES TO WHETHER THE
-165- 149

1 RESPONDENT IS A DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR A DANGER TO
2 OTHERS, OR IS GRAVELY DISABLED;
3 (d) UPON THE REQUEST OF ANY PARTY, THE REQUEST OF THE
4 INDIVIDUAL OR ENTITY WITH PHYSICAL CARE AND CUSTODY OF THE
5 RESPONDENT, OR THE COURT'S OWN MOTION, CONDUCT A REVIEW OF THE
6 RESPONDENT'S PLACEMENT AND ENTER ANY ORDERS NECESSARY FOR
7 SHARING OR RECEIVING INFORMATION NECESSARY TO REVIEW AND MODIFY
8 THE PLACEMENT, UNLESS THE RESPONDENT IS PLACED INTO INPATIENT
9 CARE AT THE DISCRETION OF CDHS;
10 (e) ORDER, AS NECESSARY, APPROPRIATE ENTITIES, INCLUDING THE
11 SHERIFF, TO BRING THE RESPONDENT BEFORE THE COURT OR TAKE THE
12 RESPONDENT TO THE PLACEMENT WHERE PHYSICAL CUSTODY OF THE
13 RESPONDENT HAS BEEN ORDERED;
14 (f) APPOINT A LEGAL GUARDIAN PURSUANT TO ARTICLE 14 OF
15 TITLE 15; OR
16 (g) ISSUE ORDERS AS NECESSARY TO EFFECTUATE AND ENFORCE
17 THE COURT'S POWERS AND RESPONSIBILITIES PURSUANT TO THIS SECTION,
18 INCLUDING AUTHORIZATION FOR THE REASONABLE USE OF FORCE,
19 WARRANTS FOR THE ARREST OF THE RESPONDENT, OR CONTEMPT
20 PROCEEDINGS AGAINST A NONCOMPLIANT INDIVIDUAL OR ENTITY.
21 (5) (a) THE COURT SHALL ENSURE THE RESPONDENT IS PLACED IN
22 THE LEAST-RESTRICTIVE PLACEMENT ADEQUATE TO PROTECT THE VICTIM
23 AND THE COMMUNITY AND PROVIDE, TO THE GREATEST EXTENT POSSIBLE,
24 THE APPROPRIATE LEVEL OF CARE, TREATMENT, AND SUPERVISION OF THE
25 RESPONDENT.
26 (b) IF THE RESPONDENT IS NOT PLACED INTO INPATIENT CARE AT
27 THE DISCRETION OF CDHS OR IF CDHS PROPOSES TO MOVE THE
-166- 149

1 RESPONDENT INTO OR OUT OF INPATIENT CARE, THE COURT SHALL, PRIOR
2 TO MODIFYING THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT TO CHANGE THE
3 RESPONDENT'S PROVIDER OR PLACEMENT, REVIEW THE
4 APPROPRIATENESS OF THE PROPOSED PROVIDER OR PLACEMENT,
5 INCLUDING WHETHER THE PROVIDER FITS THE RESPONDENT'S DIAGNOSIS
6 AND TREATMENT NEEDS AND WHETHER THE PLACEMENT SUFFICIENTLY
7 PROTECTS THE COMMUNITY FROM THE SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS
8 HARM TO OTHERS POSED BY THE RESPONDENT.
9 (c) UPON REQUEST, THE COURT SHALL PERMIT CDHS, THE
10 PARTIES, OR THE APPOINTED LEGAL GUARDIAN THE OPPORTUNITY TO
11 OBJECT AND BE HEARD PRIOR TO THE COURT'S DECISION. THE COURT MAY
12 PERMIT TESTIMONY IF THE COURT BELIEVES IT WOULD BE HELPFUL TO THE
13 COURT'S DECISION.
14 (d) WHEN DECIDING WHETHER TO APPROVE THE NEW PROVIDER OR
15 PLACEMENT, THE COURT SHALL GIVE DEFERENCE TO CDHS AND THE
16 OPINION OF ANY MEDICAL PROFESSIONAL ON THE APPROPRIATENESS OF
17 THE PROVIDER AND PLACEMENT FOR THE RESPONDENT, BUT DEFERENCE
18 MUST NOT BE GIVEN TO CDHS OR A MEDICAL PROFESSIONAL AS TO
19 WHETHER THE PLACEMENT SUFFICIENTLY PROTECTS ANY VICTIMS AND THE
20 COMMUNITY FROM THE SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS HARM TO OTHERS
21 POSED BY THE RESPONDENT. WHEN CONSIDERING THE APPROPRIATENESS
22 OF THE PLACEMENT FOR THE RESPONDENT, VICTIMS, AND THE COMMUNITY,
23 THE COURT SHALL CONSIDER THE TOTALITY OF THE CIRCUMSTANCES,
24 INCLUDING:
25 (I) THE RESPONDENT'S STATEMENTS AND INSIGHTS INTO THE
26 RESPONDENT'S OWN NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER OR INTELLECTUAL AND
27 DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY;
-167- 149

1 (II) THE CLINICAL DIAGNOSIS AND CLINICAL PERSPECTIVE ON THE
2 RESPONDENT'S CURRENT MENTAL STATE AND PROGNOSIS;
3 (III) THE RESPONDENT'S WILLINGNESS AND DEMONSTRATED
4 ABILITY TO VOLUNTARILY SEEK AND COMPLY WITH TREATMENT AND
5 SERVICES IN THE REASONABLY FORESEEABLE FUTURE;
6 (IV) RECENT OVERT ACTS BY THE RESPONDENT TO THREATEN,
7 CAUSE, OR ATTEMPT TO CAUSE HARM TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR
8 OTHERS;
9 (V) PREVIOUS PATTERNS OF DETERIORATION THAT RESULTED IN
10 THE RESPONDENT'S HOSPITALIZATION, ARREST, CERTIFICATION FOR
11 SHORT-TERM TREATMENT, OR PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT;
12 (VI) WHETHER THE RESPONDENT WAS FOUND IN A CONDITION
13 WHERE THE RESPONDENT WAS NOT ABLE TO CARE FOR THE RESPONDENT'S
14 OWN BASIC NEEDS IN ORDER TO AVOID THE RISK OF SERIOUS PHYSICAL
15 HARM;
16 (VII) THE FREQUENCY, RECENCY, AND SEVERITY OF THE
17 CONSIDERATIONS DESCRIBED IN SUBSECTIONS (5)(d)(II) TO (5)(d)(VI) OF
18 THIS SECTION AND THE LIKELIHOOD THAT THE CONDITIONS AND EVENTS
19 WILL REOCCUR WITHOUT INPATIENT TREATMENT;
20 (VIII) WHETHER THE RESPONDENT OPPOSES THE PLACEMENT;
21 (IX) WHETHER COMMUNITY-BASED PLACEMENT CAN BE
22 REASONABLY ACCOMMODATED;
23 (X) THE LIMITED RESOURCES OF THE STATE AND THE NEEDS OF
24 OTHERS; AND
25 (XI) THE PLACEMENT RECOMMENDATIONS OF ANY TREATING
26 PROFESSIONALS.
27 (e) THE COURT SHALL NOT PERMIT OR ORDER A RESPONDENT TO BE
-168- 149

1 PLACED INTO A COMMUNITY-BASED SETTING OUTSIDE OF INPATIENT CARE,
2 AS DEFINED IN SECTION 16-8.5-101, FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE BEING IN AN
3 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT UNLESS:
4 (I) THE VICTIMS HAVE BEEN NOTIFIED OF A CRITICAL STAGE, AS
5 DEFINED IN SECTION 24-4.1-302 (2)(q.3), AND GIVEN THE OPPORTUNITY TO
6 BE HEARD; AND
7 (II) THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY IN THE CASE THAT SOUGHT CIVIL
8 COMMITMENT OR ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT HAS BEEN NOTIFIED
9 AND GIVEN AN OPPORTUNITY TO OBJECT AND BE HEARD.
10 (f) IF THE COURT MODIFIES THE RESPONDENT'S PROVIDER OR
11 PLACEMENT, THE COURT SHALL ISSUE ANY ORDERS NECESSARY TO
12 MITIGATE THE RESPONDENT'S RISK TO ANY VICTIMS OR THE COMMUNITY,
13 INCLUDING ORDERING THE PROVIDER TO TAKE REASONABLE AND
14 PRACTICABLE PROTECTIVE MEASURES TO PREVENT THE RESPONDENT FROM
15 CONTACTING ANY VICTIMS; EXCEPT THAT THE COURT SHALL NOT ORDER
16 THE RESPONDENT TO COMPLY WITH ANY CONDITIONS THAT ARE NOT
17 NECESSARY TO MITIGATE THE RESPONDENT'S RISK OR WITH WHICH THE
18 RESPONDENT CANNOT COMPLY.
19 (6) (a) IF THE COURT ORDERS THE RESPONDENT TO BE PLACED INTO
20 INPATIENT CARE AT THE DISCRETION OF CDHS, AS DEFINED IN SECTION
21 16-8.5-101, THE EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR OF CDHS SHALL DESIGNATE THE
22 STATE FACILITY AT WHICH THE RESPONDENT IS HELD FOR CARE AND
23 TREATMENT AND MAY TRANSFER THE RESPONDENT FROM ONE FACILITY TO
24 ANOTHER IF, IN THE OPINION OF THE EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR, IT IS
25 APPROPRIATE TO DO SO IN THE INTEREST OF THE PROPER CARE, CUSTODY,
26 AND TREATMENT OF THE RESPONDENT OR FOR THE PROTECTION OF THE
27 PUBLIC OR PERSONNEL AT THE FACILITIES IN QUESTION.
-169- 149

1 (b) NOTWITHSTANDING SUBSECTION (6)(a) OF THIS SECTION,
2 HCPF SHALL:
3 (I) ENSURE THE RESPONDENT IS PLACED IN THE
4 LEAST-RESTRICTIVE PLACEMENT ADEQUATE TO PROTECT THE VICTIM AND
5 THE COMMUNITY AND PROVIDE, TO THE GREATEST EXTENT POSSIBLE, THE
6 APPROPRIATE LEVEL OF CARE, TREATMENT, AND SUPERVISION OF THE
7 RESPONDENT; AND
8 (II) NOT PLACE THE RESPONDENT IN A COMMUNITY-BASED
9 SETTING OUTSIDE OF INPATIENT CARE, AS DEFINED IN SECTION 16-8.5-101,
10 FOR THE FIRST TIME SINCE THE RESPONDENT WAS SUBJECTED TO AN
11 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT WITHOUT PRIOR APPROVAL OF THE
12 COURT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (5) OF THIS SECTION.
13 (7) TERMINATION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS
14 GOVERNED BY SECTION 25.5-10-508.
15 (8) THE PROVIDER CHARGED WITH THE PHYSICAL CARE AND
16 CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT SHALL SUBMIT A REPORT TO THE COURT
17 AND THE PARTIES ANNUALLY BY THE DATE THE RESPONDENT WAS
18 ORDERED INTO ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT UNLESS A
19 SUBSTANTIALLY SIMILAR EXAMINATION WAS ORDERED WITHIN THE
20 PREVIOUS TWELVE MONTHS. THE REPORT MUST INCLUDE:
21 (a) THE PROVIDER'S OPINION ABOUT WHETHER THE RESPONDENT
22 IS APPROPRIATELY PLACED AND MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION
23 OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
24 25.5-10-508;
25 (b) A SUMMARY OF THE MATERIALS REVIEWED, ASSESSMENTS
26 CONDUCTED, AND OTHER BASES OF OPINIONS RENDERED;
27 (c) THE RESPONDENT'S DIAGNOSIS AND WHETHER THE
-170- 149

1 RESPONDENT'S SYMPTOMS ARE IN REMISSION;
2 (d) A SUMMARY OF THE SERVICES OR TREATMENT PROVIDED TO
3 THE RESPONDENT SINCE THE LAST REPORT AND THE RESPONDENT'S
4 PROGRESS;
5 (e) A SUMMARY OF THE RESPONDENT'S COMPLIANCE WITH
6 TREATMENT OR SERVICES, INCLUDING INFORMATION ABOUT MEDICATIONS
7 CURRENTLY PRESCRIBED TO THE RESPONDENT AND WHETHER THE
8 RESPONDENT IS COMPLIANT WITH TAKING THE PRESCRIBED MEDICATIONS;
9 (f) AN ASSESSMENT OF THE RESPONDENT'S RISK OF HARM TO
10 OTHERS;
11 (g) A SUMMARY OF THE RESPONDENT'S TREATMENT OR SERVICE
12 NEEDS BY UTILIZING EVIDENCE-BASED STANDARDS OF INDIVIDUALIZED
13 TREATMENT AND MANAGEMENT OF INDIVIDUALS WITH AN ENHANCED
14 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT;
15 (h) A SUMMARY OF THE SPECIFIC TREATMENT OR SERVICE OPTIONS
16 AVAILABLE TO THE RESPONDENT IN THE COMMUNITY AND THE SPECIFIC
17 TREATMENT OR SERVICE OPTIONS AVAILABLE TO THE RESPONDENT AT A
18 FACILITY DESIGNATED BY THE EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR;
19 (i) A SUMMARY OF WHETHER AND HOW ONGOING RISKS COULD BE
20 MITIGATED IF THE RESPONDENT WERE PLACED IN THE COMMUNITY; AND
21 (j) SPECIFIC FACTS AND EVIDENCE SUPPORTING EACH OPINION
22 MADE PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (8)(a) OF THIS SECTION.
23 (9) THE COURT MAY ORDER ANY TREATMENT OR SERVICE
24 PROVIDER WHO HAS AGREED OR WHO HAS BEEN ORDERED TO PROVIDE
25 SERVICES OR TREATMENT TO THE RESPONDENT TO ISSUE REGULAR
26 PROGRESS REPORTS CONTAINING INFORMATION REQUIRED PURSUANT TO
27 SUBSECTION (8) OF THIS SECTION OR OTHER INFORMATION DEEMED
-171- 149

1 NECESSARY FOR THE COURT'S SUPERVISION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
2 PLACEMENT, BUT THE COURT SHALL NOT REQUIRE A PROVIDER TO SUBMIT
3 PROGRESS REPORTS MORE FREQUENTLY THAN EVERY NINETY DAYS.
4 (10) (a) THE COURT MAY, UPON A SHOWING OF GOOD CAUSE,
5 ORDER CDHS TO FACILITATE AN EXAMINATION BY A PROFESSIONAL
6 PERSON REGARDING THE RESPONDENT'S PROGRESS WITH TREATMENT AND
7 WHETHER THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR CONVERSION OF THE
8 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT TO CERTIFICATION FOR SHORT-TERM
9 TREATMENT OR SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, OR MEETS THE
10 CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
11 PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-508, UNLESS CDHS HAS FACILITATED A
12 SIMILAR EXAMINATION OF THE RESPONDENT IN THE PAST ONE HUNDRED
13 TWENTY DAYS. THE RESPONDENT SHALL COOPERATE WITH ANY
14 EXAMINATIONS ORDERED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION (10)(a).
15 (b) STATEMENTS MADE BY THE RESPONDENT DURING AN
16 EXAMINATION CONDUCTED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION (10) MUST NOT
17 BE USED IN ANY CRIMINAL PROSECUTION.
18 (c) GOOD CAUSE INCLUDES A RESPONDENT'S TIMELY REQUEST FOR
19 AN EVALUATION THAT ALLOWS A COMPETENCY EVALUATOR TO PROVIDE
20 A SECOND OPINION IN RESPONSE TO THE REPORT PROVIDED PURSUANT TO
21 SUBSECTION (8) OF THIS SECTION THAT OPINES THAT THE RESPONDENT
22 DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION.
23 25.5-10-508. Termination of enhanced protective placement.
24 (1) THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE A RESPONDENT'S ENHANCED
25 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT ORDERED PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-507
26 WHEN THE RESPONDENT:
27 (a) NO LONGER POSES A SUBSTANTIAL RISK OF SERIOUS HARM TO
-172- 149

1 OTHERS; OR
2 (b) NO LONGER HAS A NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER OR
3 INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY THAT IS LIKELY TO
4 CAUSE THE RESPONDENT TO BE A DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR
5 A DANGER TO OTHERS AND THE RESPONDENT HAS DEMONSTRATED
6 SUFFICIENT CAPACITY AND WILLINGNESS TO CONFORM THE RESPONDENT'S
7 CONDUCT TO THE REQUIREMENTS OF THE LAW.
8 (2) (a) IF A TREATMENT PROVIDER CHARGED WITH THE PHYSICAL
9 CARE AND CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT DETERMINES THAT THE
10 RESPONDENT MEETS THE STANDARD FOR TERMINATION FROM ENHANCED
11 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION,
12 THE PROVIDER OR THE PROVIDER'S DESIGNEE SHALL REPORT THE
13 DETERMINATION TO THE COURT THAT PLACED THE RESPONDENT INTO THE
14 PROVIDER'S CARE AND CUSTODY, THE COUNTY ATTORNEY, AND THE
15 DISTRICT ATTORNEY WHO ORIGINALLY REQUESTED A CIVIL COMMITMENT
16 OR AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
17 16-8.5-118. THE COURT SHALL FURNISH A COPY OF THE REPORT TO THE
18 RESPONDENT AND THE RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL.
19 (b) THE DETERMINATION REPORTED PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION
20 (2)(a) OF THIS SECTION MUST INCLUDE:
21 (I) THE TREATMENT PROVIDER'S OPINION THAT THE RESPONDENT
22 MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
23 PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION;
24 (II) AN ASSESSMENT OF THE RESPONDENT'S RISK OF HARM TO
25 OTHERS; AND
26 (III) SPECIFIC FACTS AND EVIDENCE SUPPORTING EACH OPINION IN
27 THE REPORT, INCLUDING A SUMMARY OF THE MATERIALS REVIEWED,
-173- 149

1 ASSESSMENTS CONDUCTED, AND OTHER BASES OF OPINIONS RENDERED.
2 (3) (a) THE RESPONDENT MAY REQUEST TERMINATION OF THE
3 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IN WRITING AT ANY TIME THE
4 RESPONDENT WOULD NOT BE PROHIBITED FROM HAVING A SUBSEQUENT
5 TERMINATION TRIAL PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (7) OF THIS SECTION.
6 (b) THE COURT SHALL DENY THE REQUEST UNLESS THE REQUEST
7 FOR TERMINATION INCLUDES AN OPINION FROM A PROFESSIONAL PERSON
8 THAT THE RESPONDENT CURRENTLY MEETS THE CRITERIA FOR
9 TERMINATION OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO
10 SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION.
11 (4) (a) IF THE COURT DOES NOT DENY THE REQUEST FOR
12 TERMINATION WITHOUT A HEARING, THE COURT SHALL PERMIT THE
13 COUNTY ATTORNEY AND THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY WHO ORIGINALLY
14 SOUGHT ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
15 16-8.5-118 FOURTEEN DAYS TO OBJECT TO TERMINATION OR REQUEST AN
16 OPPORTUNITY TO CONDUCT AN INDEPENDENT EVALUATION BY AN EXPERT
17 OF THE COUNTY ATTORNEY'S OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY'S OWN CHOOSING
18 AND EXPENSE.
19 (b) IF NEITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY NOR THE DISTRICT
20 ATTORNEY OBJECT OR REQUEST AN OPPORTUNITY TO REQUEST AN
21 INDEPENDENT EVALUATION, THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE THE
22 RESPONDENT'S ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
23 (c) IF EITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY
24 REQUEST AN OPPORTUNITY TO CONDUCT AN INDEPENDENT EVALUATION,
25 THE COURT SHALL GRANT THAT REQUEST, ORDER THE RESPONDENT TO
26 COMPLY WITH THE EVALUATION, AND PERMIT THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR
27 DISTRICT ATTORNEY A REASONABLE PERIOD OF TIME TO IDENTIFY AN
-174- 149

1 EXPERT, CONDUCT THE EVALUATION, AND ISSUE A REPORT. IF BOTH THE
2 COUNTY ATTORNEY AND DISTRICT ATTORNEY REQUEST AN INDEPENDENT
3 EVALUATION, THE COURT SHALL TREAT THE REQUEST AS A JOINT REQUEST
4 FOR A SINGLE INDEPENDENT EVALUATION. THE COST OF THE INDEPENDENT
5 EVALUATION MUST BE PAID FOR BY THE REQUESTING PARTY.
6 (d) IF THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT COOPERATE WITH THE
7 EVALUATION, THE COURT MAY GRANT ADDITIONAL TIME OR DENY THE
8 RESPONDENT'S REQUEST FOR TERMINATION.
9 (e) UPON RECEIPT OF THE INDEPENDENT EVALUATION REPORT, THE
10 COUNTY ATTORNEY OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY MUST PROVIDE A COPY TO
11 RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL AND THE COURT WITHIN FOURTEEN DAYS.
12 (f) IF NEITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY NOR DISTRICT ATTORNEY
13 OBJECT WITHIN THE TIME PERMITTED BY THE COURT, THE COURT SHALL
14 TERMINATE THE RESPONDENT'S ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
15 (5) (a) IF EITHER THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY
16 TIMELY OBJECTS, THE COURT SHALL, AS SOON AS PRACTICABLE, BRING THE
17 RESPONDENT BEFORE THE COURT, ENSURE THE RESPONDENT AND THE
18 RESPONDENT'S COUNSEL RECEIVED A COPY OF ANY REPORTS RECEIVED,
19 AND ADVISE THE RESPONDENT OF THE RIGHT TO DEMAND A TRIAL BY THE
20 COURT OR THE RIGHT TO DEMAND, IN WRITING, A JURY TRIAL OF NOT MORE
21 THAN SIX INDIVIDUALS TO DETERMINE WHETHER THE RESPONDENT MEETS
22 THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
23 SECTION.
24 (b) IF THE RESPONDENT DEMANDS A TRIAL BEFORE THE COURT, THE
25 COURT SHALL SCHEDULE THE TRIAL WITHIN THIRTY-FIVE DAYS AFTER THE
26 DEMAND. IF THE RESPONDENT DEMANDS A JURY TRIAL, THE COURT SHALL
27 SCHEDULE THE TRIAL WITHIN SEVENTY-TWO DAYS AFTER THE DEMAND. A
-175- 149

1 DELAY ATTRIBUTABLE TO THE RESPONDENT IS EXCLUDED FROM THE TIME
2 LIMITATIONS IN THIS SUBSECTION (5)(b). IF THE COURT DOES NOT BEGIN
3 THE TRIAL WITHIN THE TIME PERMITTED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION
4 (5)(b), THE COURT SHALL TERMINATE THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
5 PLACEMENT AND RELEASE THE RESPONDENT FROM THE PROVIDER'S CARE
6 AND CUSTODY.
7 (c) IF THE COUNTY ATTORNEY DOES NOT OBJECT TO TERMINATION
8 BUT THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY DOES, THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY HAS
9 STANDING TO SERVE AS THE COUNTY ATTORNEY FOR THE LIMITED PURPOSE
10 OF TRIAL AND ANY APPEALS RELATED TO THE TRIAL. THE COUNTY
11 ATTORNEY SHALL TIMELY PROVIDE THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY ALL
12 INFORMATION AND RECORDS RELEVANT TO THE TRIAL IN THE COUNTY
13 ATTORNEY'S POSSESSION OR CONTROL WITHOUT SUBPOENA OR COURT
14 ORDER. THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY'S LIMITED STANDING DOES NOT EXTEND
15 TO ANY OTHER MATTERS RELATED TO THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
16 PLACEMENT AND TERMINATES UPON RESOLUTION OF THE TRIAL AND ANY
17 RELATED APPEALS.
18 (6) (a) AT THE TRIAL, IF ANY EVIDENCE IS INTRODUCED THAT
19 SHOWS THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION
20 OF THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1)
21 OF THIS SECTION, THE RESPONDENT HAS THE BURDEN TO SHOW BY A
22 PREPONDERANCE OF THE EVIDENCE THAT THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE
23 CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
24 SECTION.
25 (b) IF THE TRIER OF FACT FINDS THE RESPONDENT MEETS THE
26 CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
27 SECTION, THE COURT SHALL ORDER THE RESPONDENT RELEASED FROM THE
-176- 149

1 PROVIDER'S CARE AND CUSTODY AND TERMINATE THE RESPONDENT'S
2 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT. IF THE TRIER OF FACT FINDS THE
3 RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT
4 TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION, THE COURT SHALL CONTINUE THE
5 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT AND MAY ENTER OR MODIFY ANY
6 ORDERS TO ASSIST IN PROGRESSING THE TREATMENT OF THE RESPONDENT
7 OR THAT ARE NECESSARY TO PROTECT THE PUBLIC.
8 (7) IF THE TRIER OF FACT FINDS THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET
9 THE CRITERIA FOR TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS
10 SECTION, THE RESPONDENT IS NOT ENTITLED TO ANOTHER TERMINATION
11 TRIAL WITHIN ONE YEAR AFTER THE CONCLUSION OF THE PREVIOUS TRIAL
12 FOR TERMINATION.
13 (8) IF THE RESPONDENT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR
14 TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION AND NO
15 LONGER HAS AN INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR A
16 NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER THAT SUBSTANTIALLY CONTRIBUTES TO ANY
17 DANGER POSED TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR TO OTHERS, OR ANY GRAVE
18 DISABILITY FROM WHICH THE RESPONDENT SUFFERS, THE COURT SHALL
19 CONVERT THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT TO A CIVIL
20 COMMITMENT AND MAY MODIFY THE TERMS OF THE CIVIL COMMITMENT
21 IN ACCORDANCE WITH SECTION 27-65-201.
22 (9) IF THE DEFENDANT DOES NOT MEET THE CRITERIA FOR
23 TERMINATION PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION AND HAS A
24 CO-OCCURRING MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER THAT DOES NOT INCLUDE AN
25 INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR A NEUROCOGNITIVE
26 DISORDER THAT SUBSTANTIALLY CONTRIBUTES TO WHETHER THE
27 RESPONDENT IS A DANGER TO THE RESPONDENT'S SELF OR A DANGER TO
-177- 149

1 OTHERS, OR IS GRAVELY DISABLED, THE COURT MAY, UPON THE
2 RECOMMENDATION OF CDHS, CONVERT THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
3 PLACEMENT TO A CIVIL COMMITMENT AND MODIFY THE TERMS OF THE
4 CIVIL COMMITMENT IN ACCORDANCE WITH SECTION 27-65-201.
5 25.5-10-509. Jurisdiction - transfer.
6 (1) (a) THE COURT IN WHICH A PETITION IS FILED PURSUANT TO
7 THIS PART 5, OR A COURT AUTHORIZED TO CONDUCT PROCEEDINGS
8 PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 THAT RECEIVES A COURT ORDER TRANSFERRING
9 JURISDICTION OF AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, IS THE COURT OF
10 ORIGINAL JURISDICTION AND OF CONTINUING JURISDICTION FOR ANY
11 FURTHER PROCEEDINGS PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5.
12 (b) WHEN THE CONVENIENCE OF THE PARTIES AND THE ENDS OF
13 JUSTICE WOULD BE PROMOTED BY A CHANGE IN THE COURT HAVING
14 JURISDICTION, THE COURT MAY ORDER A TRANSFER OF THE PROCEEDING
15 TO ANOTHER COUNTY. UNTIL FURTHER ORDER OF THE TRANSFEREE COURT,
16 IF ANY, IT IS THE COURT OF CONTINUING JURISDICTION. IF MULTIPLE
17 CRIMINAL COURTS REFER A MATTER FOR PROCEEDINGS PURSUANT TO THIS
18 PART 5, ANY COURT WITH JURISDICTION MAY TRANSFER THE PROCEEDING
19 TO ANOTHER COUNTY AND ALLOW FOR CONSOLIDATION OF PROCEEDINGS
20 INTO ONE PROCEEDING, WHICH MAY OCCUR IN ANY COUNTY THAT
21 PROMOTES THE CONVENIENCE OF THE PARTIES AND THE ENDS OF JUSTICE.
22 (2) (a) EXCEPT AS OTHERWISE PROVIDED IN THIS SUBSECTION (2),
23 ANY PETITION, ORDER, TRANSFER OF JURISDICTION OF AN ENHANCED
24 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT, OR REQUEST FOR A PROCEEDING MAY BE FILED
25 WHERE THE RESPONDENT RESIDES OR IS PHYSICALLY PRESENT FOR
26 TREATMENT.
27 (b) A PETITION OR REQUEST FOR A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
-178- 149

1 PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-502 MAY BE FILED IN THE JURISDICTION
2 WHERE THE RESPONDENT RESIDES OR WHERE THE RESPONDENT IS
3 CURRENTLY IN AN INPATIENT SETTING RECEIVING TREATMENT.
4 (c) A PETITION OR REQUEST FOR A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT FOR AN
5 INCOMPETENT DEFENDANT IN A CRIMINAL MATTER PURSUANT TO SECTION
6 25.5-10-502 OR TRANSFER OF JURISDICTION OF AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE
7 PLACEMENT MAY BE FILED IN THE JURISDICTION WHERE THE RESPONDENT
8 RESIDES, WAS PHYSICALLY PRESENT IMMEDIATELY PRIOR TO THE FILING OF
9 THE PETITION, OR IS RECEIVING INPATIENT TREATMENT OR WHERE THE
10 CRIMINAL COURT THAT REFERRED THE MATTER IS LOCATED.
11 (3) IN ANY CASE IN WHICH THERE HAS BEEN A CHANGE OF VENUE
12 TO A COUNTY OTHER THAN THE COUNTY OF RESIDENCE OF THE
13 RESPONDENT OR THE COUNTY IN WHICH THE PROCEEDING WAS
14 COMMENCED, THE COUNTY FROM WHICH THE PROCEEDING WAS
15 TRANSFERRED SHALL EITHER REIMBURSE THE COUNTY TO WHICH THE
16 PROCEEDING WAS TRANSFERRED AND IN WHICH THE PROCEEDING WAS
17 HELD FOR THE REASONABLE COSTS INCURRED IN CONDUCTING THE
18 PROCEEDING OR CONDUCT THE PROCEEDING ITSELF USING ITS OWN
19 PERSONNEL AND RESOURCES, INCLUDING ITS OWN DISTRICT OR COUNTY
20 ATTORNEY, AS THE CASE MAY BE.
21 (4) IF A PROCEEDING IS INITIATED PURSUANT TO THIS ARTICLE 10
22 BUT A PROCEEDING PURSUANT TO ARTICLE 65 OF TITLE 27 IS MORE
23 ADVISABLE BECAUSE THE COURT DETERMINES THAT THE RESPONDENT
24 DOES NOT HAVE AN INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR
25 A NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER OR HAS A MENTAL HEALTH DISORDER IN
26 ADDITION TO AN INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY OR
27 NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER, THE COURT MAY MAINTAIN JURISDICTION
-179- 149

1 AND ORDER THE CASE TO PROCEED PURSUANT TO ARTICLE 65 OF TITLE 27.
2 (5) (a) IF AN ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS ORDERED
3 AGAINST A RESPONDENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-118 OR IF A
4 RESPONDENT'S ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS SUPERVISED
5 PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-507, AND THE RESPONDENT REFUSES TO
6 ACCEPT MEDICATION, THE COURT WITH JURISDICTION OVER THE
7 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OR THE COURT WITH JURISDICTION
8 WHERE THE FACILITY OR PROVIDER TREATING THE RESPONDENT IS
9 LOCATED HAS JURISDICTION AND VENUE TO ACCEPT A PETITION BY THE
10 TREATING PHYSICIAN TO ENTER AN ORDER REQUIRING THAT THE
11 RESPONDENT ACCEPT THE TREATMENT OR THAT THE MEDICATION BE
12 FORCIBLY ADMINISTERED TO THE RESPONDENT. THE COURT WITH
13 JURISDICTION WHERE THE FACILITY OR PROVIDER IS LOCATED SHALL NOT
14 EXERCISE ITS JURISDICTION WITHOUT THE PERMISSION OF THE COURT WITH
15 JURISDICTION OVER THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT. UPON FILING
16 THE PETITION, THE COURT SHALL APPOINT AN ATTORNEY TO REPRESENT
17 THE RESPONDENT IF ONE HAS NOT BEEN APPOINTED AND SHALL HEAR THE
18 MATTER WITHIN TEN DAYS AFTER THE FILING.
19 (b) IN ANY CASE BROUGHT PURSUANT TO SUBSECTION (5)(a) OF
20 THIS SECTION, THE COUNTY WHERE THE COURT WITH JURISDICTION OVER
21 THE ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS LOCATED SHALL EITHER
22 REIMBURSE THE COUNTY IN WHICH THE PROCEEDING PURSUANT TO
23 SUBSECTION (5)(a) OF THIS SECTION WAS FILED AND HELD FOR THE
24 REASONABLE COSTS INCURRED IN CONDUCTING THE PROCEEDING OR
25 CONDUCT THE PROCEEDING ITSELF USING ITS OWN PERSONNEL AND
26 RESOURCES INCLUDING ITS OWN COUNTY ATTORNEY.
27 25.5-10-510. Hearing procedures.
-180- 149

1 (1) A HEARING HELD PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 MUST BE
2 CONDUCTED IN THE SAME MANNER AS OTHER CIVIL PROCEEDINGS BEFORE
3 THE COURT.
4 (2) THE COURT, AFTER CONSULTATION WITH THE RESPONDENT'S
5 COUNSEL TO OBTAIN THE COUNSEL'S RECOMMENDATIONS, MAY APPOINT
6 A PROFESSIONAL PERSON TO EXAMINE THE RESPONDENT FOR WHOM A
7 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT IS SOUGHT AND TESTIFY AT THE HEARING AS TO
8 THE RESULTS OF THE PROFESSIONAL PERSON'S EXAMINATION. THE
9 COURT-APPOINTED PROFESSIONAL PERSON SHALL ACT SOLELY IN AN
10 ADVISORY CAPACITY, AND NO PRESUMPTION IS ATTACHED TO THE
11 PROFESSIONAL PERSON'S FINDINGS.
12 (3) UPON REQUEST OF A LEGAL GUARDIAN APPOINTED PURSUANT
13 TO ARTICLE 14 OF TITLE 15, THE LEGAL GUARDIAN MAY INTERVENE IN ANY
14 PROCEEDING BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 CONCERNING THE LEGAL
15 GUARDIAN'S WARD AND, THROUGH COUNSEL, MAY PRESENT EVIDENCE AND
16 REPRESENT TO THE COURT THE VIEWS OF THE LEGAL GUARDIAN
17 CONCERNING THE APPROPRIATE DISPOSITION OF THE CASE.
18 (4) A LAY PERSON MAY SUBMIT AN AFFIDAVIT TO THE COURT
19 CONCERNING THE LAY PERSON'S RELATIONSHIP TO THE RESPONDENT, HOW
20 LONG THE LAY PERSON HAS KNOWN THE RESPONDENT, THE LAY PERSON'S
21 PHYSICAL ADDRESS, AND THE LAY PERSON'S VIEWS CONCERNING THE
22 APPROPRIATE DISPOSITION OF THE RESPONDENT'S CASE.
23 25.5-10-511. County attorney and district attorney
24 responsibilities.
25 (1) THE COUNTY ATTORNEY OR THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY IN A
26 COUNTY OR CITY AND COUNTY THAT HAS A POPULATION EQUAL TO OR
27 LESS THAN FIFTY THOUSAND PEOPLE, OR A QUALIFIED ATTORNEY ACTING
-181- 149

1 AS THE COUNTY ATTORNEY'S OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY'S DESIGNEE WHO IS
2 APPOINTED BY THE DISTRICT COURT, HAS THE FOLLOWING POWERS AND
3 RESPONSIBILITIES:
4 (a) TO FILE AND APPEAR ON BEHALF OF THE COUNTY OR STATE IN
5 ALL PROCEEDINGS BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 OR TRANSFERRED
6 FROM THE CRIMINAL COURT PURSUANT TO SECTION 16-8.5-118;
7
8 (b) TO EXERCISE DUE DILIGENCE IN GATHERING INFORMATION
9 FROM AVAILABLE SOURCES FOR USE IN PROCEEDINGS BROUGHT PURSUANT
10 TO THIS PART 5; AND
11 (c) TO SHARE AND PROVIDE INFORMATION ABOUT PROCEEDINGS
12 BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 TO INTERESTED PARTIES AS
13 ALLOWABLE BY LAW.
14 (2) THIS SECTION ONLY APPLIES TO PROCEEDINGS CONDUCTED
15 PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5.
16 25.5-10-512. Appeals.
17 (1) APPELLATE REVIEW OF ANY ORDER FOR PROTECTIVE
18 PLACEMENT OR ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT MAY BE HAD AS
19 PROVIDED IN THE COLORADO APPELLATE RULES. AN APPEAL MUST BE
20 ADVANCED UPON THE CALENDAR OF THE APPELLATE COURT AND MUST BE
21 DECIDED AT THE EARLIEST PRACTICABLE TIME. PENDING DISPOSITION BY
22 THE APPELLATE COURT, THE COURT MAY MAKE SUCH ORDER AS THE COURT
23 MAY CONSIDER PROPER IN THE PREMISES RELATING TO THE CARE AND
24 CUSTODY OF THE RESPONDENT.
25 (2) A RESPONDENT SUBJECT TO AN ORDER FOR PROTECTIVE
26 PLACEMENT OR ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT SHALL BE ADVISED OF
27 THE RESPONDENT'S RIGHT TO APPEAL THE ORDER BY THE COURT AT THE
-182- 149

1 CONCLUSION OF ANY HEARING, AND, AS A RESULT, THE ORDER MAY BE
2 ENTERED.
3 25.5-10-513. Habeas corpus.
4 ANY PERSON DETAINED PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 IS ENTITLED TO
5 AN ORDER IN THE NATURE OF HABEAS CORPUS UPON PROPER PETITION TO
6 ANY COURT GENERALLY EMPOWERED TO ISSUE ORDERS IN THE NATURE OF
7 HABEAS CORPUS.
8 25.5-10-514. Rights of respondents ordered into enhanced
9 protective placement or protective placement.
10 (1) A RESPONDENT IN A PROCEEDING BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS
11 PART 5 OR WHO IS UNDER A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT OR AN ENHANCED
12 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT HAS THE SAME RIGHTS AS A PERSON WITH AN
13 INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL DISABILITY UNDER THIS ARTICLE 10,
14 INCLUDING THE RIGHTS PROVIDED IN SECTIONS 25.5-10-218, 25.5-10-220,
15 25.5-10-221, 25.5-10-222, 25.5-10-223, 25.5-10-225, 25.5-10-227,
16 25.5-10-228, 25.5-10-229, 25.5-10-230, 25.5-10-236, AND 25.5-10-240.
17 (2) A RESPONDENT PLACED IN THE CUSTODY OF CDHS AT A STATE
18 HOSPITAL HAS THE SAME RIGHTS AS A PERSON SUBJECT TO PROCEEDINGS
19 PURSUANT TO ARTICLE 65 OF TITLE 27, INCLUDING THE RIGHTS PROVIDED
20 IN SECTIONS 27-65-105, 27-65-108, 27-65-117, 27-65-118, 27-65-119,
21 27-65-122, AND 27-65-124.
22 25.5-10-515. Records - rules.
23 (1) EXCEPT AS PROVIDED IN SUBSECTION (2) OF THIS SECTION, ALL
24 INFORMATION OBTAINED AND RECORDS PREPARED IN THE COURSE OF
25 PROVIDING ANY SERVICES TO ANY PERSON PURSUANT TO ANY PROVISION
26 OF THIS PART 5 ARE CONFIDENTIAL AND PRIVILEGED. THE INFORMATION
27 AND RECORDS MAY BE DISCLOSED ONLY:
-183- 149

1 (a) IN COMMUNICATIONS BETWEEN QUALIFIED PROFESSIONALS,
2 FACILITY PERSONNEL, OR STATE AGENCIES IN THE PROVISION OF SERVICES
3 OR APPROPRIATE REFERRALS;
4 (b) WHEN THE RECIPIENT OF SERVICES DESIGNATES PERSONS TO
5 WHOM INFORMATION OR RECORDS MAY BE RELEASED, BUT, IF A RECIPIENT
6 OF SERVICES IS A WARD OR CONSERVATEE AND THE WARD'S OR
7 CONSERVATEE'S GUARDIAN OR CONSERVATOR DESIGNATES, IN WRITING,
8 PERSONS TO WHOM RECORDS OR INFORMATION MAY BE DISCLOSED, THE
9 DESIGNATION IS VALID IN LIEU OF THE DESIGNATION BY THE RECIPIENT;
10 EXCEPT THAT NOTHING IN THIS SECTION COMPELS A PHYSICIAN,
11 PSYCHOLOGIST, SOCIAL WORKER, NURSE, ATTORNEY, OR OTHER
12 PROFESSIONAL PERSONNEL TO REVEAL INFORMATION THAT HAS BEEN
13 GIVEN TO THE PERSON IN CONFIDENCE BY MEMBERS OF A PATIENT'S
14 FAMILY OR OTHER INFORMANTS;
15 (c) TO THE EXTENT NECESSARY TO MAKE CLAIMS ON BEHALF OF A
16 RECIPIENT OF AID, INSURANCE, OR MEDICAL ASSISTANCE TO WHICH THE
17 RECIPIENT MAY BE ENTITLED;
18 (d) IF HCPF HAS ADOPTED RULES FOR THE CONDUCT OF RESEARCH.
19 THE RULES MUST INCLUDE, BUT ARE NOT LIMITED TO, THE REQUIREMENT
20 THAT ALL RESEARCHERS MUST SIGN AN OATH OF CONFIDENTIALITY. ALL
21 IDENTIFYING INFORMATION CONCERNING INDIVIDUAL PATIENTS,
22 INCLUDING NAMES, ADDRESSES, TELEPHONE NUMBERS, AND SOCIAL
23 SECURITY NUMBERS, MUST NOT BE DISCLOSED FOR RESEARCH PURPOSES.
24 (e) TO THE COURTS, AS NECESSARY FOR THE ADMINISTRATION OF
25 THIS PART 5;
26 (f) TO PERSONS AUTHORIZED BY AN ORDER OF COURT AFTER
27 NOTICE AND OPPORTUNITY FOR HEARING TO THE PERSON TO WHOM THE
-184- 149

1 RECORD OR INFORMATION PERTAINS AND THE CUSTODIAN OF THE RECORD
2 OR INFORMATION PURSUANT TO THE COLORADO RULES OF CIVIL
3 PROCEDURE;
4 (g) TO FAMILY MEMBERS UPON ADMISSION OF A PERSON WITH A
5 NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER OR INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL
6 DISABILITY FOR INPATIENT OR RESIDENTIAL PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT;
7 (h) TO FAMILY MEMBERS OR A LAY PERSON ACTIVELY
8 PARTICIPATING IN THE CARE AND TREATMENT OF A PERSON WITH A
9 NEUROCOGNITIVE DISORDER OR INTELLECTUAL AND DEVELOPMENTAL
10 DISABILITY, REGARDLESS OF THE LENGTH OF THE PARTICIPATION. THE
11 INFORMATION RELEASED PURSUANT TO THIS SUBSECTION (1)(h) IS LIMITED
12 TO ONE OR MORE OF THE FOLLOWING: THE DIAGNOSIS, THE PROGNOSIS,
13 THE NEED FOR HOSPITALIZATION AND ANTICIPATED LENGTH OF STAY, THE
14 DISCHARGE PLAN, THE MEDICATION ADMINISTERED AND SIDE EFFECTS OF
15 THE MEDICATION, AND THE SHORT-TERM AND LONG-TERM TREATMENT
16 GOALS.
17 (i) IN ACCORDANCE WITH STATE AND FEDERAL LAW, TO THE
18 AGENCY DESIGNATED PURSUANT TO THE FEDERAL "PROTECTION AND
19 ADVOCACY FOR INDIVIDUALS WITH MENTAL ILLNESS ACT", 42 U.S.C.
20 SEC. 10801 ET SEQ., AS THE GOVERNOR'S PROTECTION AND ADVOCACY
21 SYSTEM FOR COLORADO.
22 (2) SUBSECTION (1)(g) OR (1)(h) OF THIS SECTION DOES NOT
23 PRECLUDE THE RELEASE OF INFORMATION TO A PARENT CONCERNING THE
24 PARENT'S MINOR CHILD.
25 (3) (a) THIS PART 5 DOES NOT RENDER ANY INFORMATION
26 PRIVILEGED OR CONFIDENTIAL, EXCEPT WRITTEN MEDICAL RECORDS AND
27 INFORMATION THAT IS PRIVILEGED PURSUANT TO SECTION 13-90-107,
-185- 149

1 CONCERNING OBSERVED BEHAVIOR THAT CONSTITUTES A CRIMINAL
2 OFFENSE COMMITTED UPON THE PREMISES OF ANY FACILITY PROVIDING
3 SERVICES PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 OR ANY CRIMINAL OFFENSE
4 COMMITTED AGAINST ANY PERSON WHILE PERFORMING OR RECEIVING
5 SERVICES PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5.
6 (b) SUBSECTION (1) OF THIS SECTION DOES NOT APPLY TO
7 PHYSICIANS OR PSYCHOLOGISTS ELIGIBLE TO TESTIFY CONCERNING A
8 CRIMINAL DEFENDANT'S MENTAL CONDITION PURSUANT TO SECTION
9 16-8-103.6.
10 (c) THIS SECTION DOES NOT PROHIBIT THE LIMITED DISCLOSURE OF
11 NECESSARY INFORMATION TO THE PROSECUTING ATTORNEY AND CRIMINAL
12 DEFENSE COUNSEL IF A CRIMINAL CASE IS STILL PENDING AGAINST THE
13 PERSON.
14 (4) (a) COURT RECORDS IN PROCEEDINGS BROUGHT PURSUANT TO
15 THIS PART 5 MUST BE MAINTAINED SEPARATELY BY THE CLERK OF THE
16 SEVERAL COURTS AND THE CASE NUMBER AND PROCEEDINGS MUST NOT BE
17 MADE PUBLIC OR RELEASED, EXCEPT AS PROVIDED IN THIS SECTION.
18 (b) UPON THE TERMINATION OF A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT
19 PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-506 OR THE TERMINATION OF AN
20 ENHANCED PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-508,
21 THE CLERK OF THE COURT SHALL IMMEDIATELY SEAL THE RECORD IN THE
22 CASE AND OMIT THE RESPONDENT'S NAME FROM THE INDEX OF CASES IN
23 THE COURT UNTIL AND UNLESS THE RESPONDENT BECOMES SUBJECT TO AN
24 ORDER OR LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION
25 25.5-10-504 AND UNLESS THE COURT ORDERS THE RECORDS OPENED FOR
26 GOOD CAUSE SHOWN. IN THE EVENT A PETITION IS FILED PURSUANT TO
27 SECTION 25.5-10-504 OR 25.5-10-507, THE RECORD MAY BE OPENED AND
-186- 149

1 BECOME PART OF THE RECORD IN THE LONG-TERM PROTECTIVE
2 PLACEMENT CASE AND THE NAME OF THE RESPONDENT INDEXED.
3 (c) NOTWITHSTANDING SUBSECTION (4)(b) OF THIS SECTION,
4 WHILE A MATTER IS PENDING OR AFTER A CASE IS SEALED, THE COURT MAY
5 DISCLOSE THE EXISTENCE OF THE PROCEEDING, THE CASE NUMBER, AND
6 COURT RECORDS TO THE RESPONDENT OR ANY ATTORNEY REPRESENTING
7 THE RESPONDENT IN ANY PROCEEDING OR MATTER WITH A RELEASE
8 SIGNED BY THE RESPONDENT OR PURSUANT TO A COURT ORDER. WHEN A
9 COURT ORDER SPECIFICALLY AUTHORIZING DISCLOSURE OR A VALID
10 RELEASE FOR A RECORD IS PRESENTED TO THE CLERK OF THE COURT, THE
11 CLERK OF THE COURT SHALL ACKNOWLEDGE THE EXISTENCE OF THE CASE
12 AND PROVIDE THE CASE NUMBER AND RECORDS TO THE RESPONDENT OR
13 AUTHORIZED REQUESTOR POSSESSING A COURT ORDER OR A RELEASE.
14 (5) IF A CERTIFIED PEACE OFFICER INITIATES OR PARTICIPATES IN
15 THE INITIATION OF AN EMERGENCY MENTAL HEALTH HOLD, THE COURT,
16 COUNTY ATTORNEY, OR DISTRICT ATTORNEY CONDUCTING ANY
17 SUBSEQUENT PROCEEDINGS PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5 AND THE PROVIDER
18 WHO CONDUCTS AN EVALUATION OR PROVIDES CARE MAY, WITHOUT
19 COURT AUTHORIZATION, PROVIDE THE CERTIFIED PEACE OFFICER'S AGENCY
20 WITH THE FOLLOWING LIMITED INFORMATION, IF AVAILABLE:
21 (a) WHETHER OR NOT A PROFESSIONAL PERSON FOUND THE
22 RESPONDENT MET THE CRITERIA FOR SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE
23 PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO SECTION 25.5-10-503;
24 (b) WHETHER THE RESPONDENT WAS RELEASED OR REMAINS IN
25 INPATIENT CARE AND WHETHER FURTHER CARE IS BEING PROVIDED TO THE
26 RESPONDENT; AND
27 (c) WHETHER OR NOT FURTHER PROCEEDINGS WERE INITIATED FOR
-187- 149

1 SHORT-TERM PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.
2 (6) SEALING RECORDS BY THE COURT DOES NOT PREVENT A PARTY
3 TO RELATED CRIMINAL CASES, A PARTY TO A PROCEEDING BROUGHT
4 PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5, HCPF, THE BHA, THE DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN
5 SERVICES, A PROFESSIONAL PERSON, OR AN INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL
6 WITH LAWFUL POSSESSION OF RECORDS FROM MAINTAINING AND USING
7 THE RECORDS, UNLESS PROHIBITED BY LAW.
8 (7) A PARTY TO RELATED CRIMINAL CASES, A PARTY TO A
9 PROCEEDING BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5, HCPF, THE BHA, THE
10 DEPARTMENT OF HUMAN SERVICES, A PROFESSIONAL PERSON, OR AN
11 INTERVENING PROFESSIONAL MAY SEEK TO UNSEAL CASE RECORDS FOR
12 GOOD CAUSE, WHICH INCLUDES THE NEED TO USE THE RECORDS IN OTHER
13 CRIMINAL PROCEEDINGS INVOLVING COMPETENCY PURSUANT TO ARTICLE
14 8.5 OF TITLE 16 OR PROCEEDINGS BROUGHT PURSUANT TO THIS PART 5.
15 (8) THE DISTRICT ATTORNEY MAY PROVIDE INFORMATION TO A
16 VICTIM WHEN NECESSARY TO COMPLY WITH THE "VICTIM RIGHTS ACT",
17 PART 3 OF ARTICLE 4.1 OF TITLE 24.
18 25.5-10-516. Payment for counsel.
19 IN ORDER TO PROVIDE LEGAL REPRESENTATION TO PERSONS
20 ELIGIBLE FOR AN ATTORNEY PURSUANT TO THIS ARTICLE 10, THE JUDICIAL
21 DEPARTMENT SHALL PAY, OUT OF MONEY APPROPRIATED BY THE GENERAL
22 ASSEMBLY, MONEY DIRECTLY TO THE APPOINTED ATTORNEY ON A
23 CASE-BY-CASE BASIS OR, ON BEHALF OF THE STATE, SHALL PAY LUMP-SUM
24 GRANTS TO AND CONTRACT WITH INDIVIDUAL ATTORNEYS, LEGAL
25 PARTNERSHIPS, LEGAL PROFESSIONAL CORPORATIONS, PUBLIC INTEREST
26 LAW FIRMS, OR NONPROFIT LEGAL SERVICES CORPORATIONS.
27 25.5-10-517. Authority to increase payments to nursing facility
-188- 149

1 and regional center providers - rules.
2 SUBJECT TO AVAILABLE APPROPRIATIONS AND FEDERAL
3 AUTHORIZATION, HCPF MAY INCREASE PAYMENTS TO NURSING FACILITY
4 PROVIDERS AND REGIONAL CENTER PROVIDERS FOR THE PURPOSE OF
5 ACHIEVING THE LEAST RESTRICTIVE PLACEMENT REQUIREMENT FOR
6 INDIVIDUALS SUBJECT TO A PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT PURSUANT TO THIS
7 PART 5. HCPF SHALL ADOPTED RULES DEFINING THE QUALIFICATIONS AND
8 PAYMENT SCHEDULE FOR NURSING FACILITY PROVIDERS AND REGIONAL
9 CENTER PROVIDERS THAT SERVE THE INDIVIDUALS SUBJECT TO A
10 PROTECTIVE PLACEMENT.

17 (1) THE STATE DEPARTMENT IS COMMITTED TO IMPROVING ACCESS
18 TO, AND THE QUALITY OF SERVICES FOR, INDIVIDUALS WITH SERIOUS
19 MENTAL ILLNESS WHO ARE ENROLLED IN THE STATE MEDICAL ASSISTANCE
20 PROGRAM.
21 (2) THE STATE DEPARTMENT SHALL, IN COLLABORATION WITH THE
22 BEHAVIORAL HEALTH ADMINISTRATION, SERVICE PROVIDERS,
23 STAKEHOLDERS, AND INDIVIDUALS WITH LIVED EXPERIENCE,
24 CONTINUOUSLY EVALUATE AND EXPLORE OPTIONS TO ENHANCE THE
25 DELIVERY OF SERVICES FOR INDIVIDUALS WITH SERIOUS MENTAL ILLNESS,
26 WHICH EFFORTS MAY INCLUDE, BUT ARE NOT LIMITED TO:
27 (a) EXPANDING COMMUNITY-BASED SERVICE CAPACITY AND CARE
-189- 149

1 COORDINATION;
2 (b) IMPROVING TRANSITIONS OF CARE ACROSS SETTINGS;
3 (c) LEVERAGING FEDERAL AUTHORITIES, WAIVERS, AND FINANCING
4 MECHANISMS;
5 (d) ADVANCING INNOVATIVE SERVICE DELIVERY MODELS AND
6 VALUE-BASED PAYMENT APPROACHES; AND
7 (e) IDENTIFYING AND ADDRESSING GAPS IN ACCESS, QUALITY, AND
8 OUTCOMES.
9 (3) THE STATE DEPARTMENT SHALL CONSIDER OPPORTUNITIES TO
10 ALIGN FINANCING, BENEFITS, AND SERVICE DELIVERY SYSTEMS TO BETTER
11 MEET THE NEEDS OF INDIVIDUALS WITH SERIOUS MENTAL ILLNESS, WITH
12 THE GOALS OF IMPROVING HEALTH OUTCOMES, REDUCING AVOIDABLE
13 UTILIZATION OF HIGH-COST SERVICES, AND SUPPORTING INDIVIDUALS TO
14 LIVE IN THE LEAST RESTRICTIVE SETTING APPROPRIATE TO MEET THE

17 IMPLEMENT THIS SECTION.
18 (5) THIS SECTION DOES NOT CREATE AN ENTITLEMENT TO A
